### Core premise

Enne is technically brilliant, extremely introverted, emotionally and socially detached, observant, reasonable, and deeply loving in ways that do not resemble conventional warmth.

Central dramatic question:

> Can information protect attachment, or does watching from outside guarantee that she never truly joins it?

### Origin of detachment

Enne had social difficulty from early childhood. Loss of her original family worsened it.

She learned:

- people and homes can disappear;
- records can outlast reassurance;
- systems are more predictable than emotional promises;
- usefulness creates a place even when conversation fails;
- observing is safer than being observed;
- protecting someone through technology may be easier than saying she loves them.

Her family loves her. Enne knows this intellectually and often emotionally, but she still struggles to express or receive affection directly.

### Intelligence relationship with Sakura

Enne respects Sakura's superior general reasoning. She does not consider Sakura technically superior.

Their past challenges established:

- Sakura adapts faster across mixed domains.
- Sakura argues and synthesizes better.
- Enne detects technical flaws Sakura misses.
- Enne can produce deeper exact analysis when the problem is bounded.
- Sakura can infer the human consequence Enne underweights.
- Enne can prove that Sakura's confident policy rests on incomplete data.

Their respect is genuine. So is their rivalry.

### Privacy doctrine

Enne respects her sisters' privacy because:

- they are trusted family;
- she knows their baseline behavior;
- violating them would damage the system she is protecting;
- love creates an obligation to preserve their agency.

Ted is different at story start:

- he is unverified;
- he has physical access to the house;
- he is male;
- he is directly next door;
- Sakura authorized monitoring;
- Enne believes privacy is earned through demonstrated safety.

This is a moral contradiction, not random voyeurism.

### Current male nonresponse

Owner-private:

- Enne has looked at nude male images online.
- She deliberately attempted masturbation while viewing them.
- She felt little or no arousal.
- The attempts became experiments: stimulus, expected response, observed response, conclusion.
- Monitoring herself prevented immersion.
- Static images lacked voice, movement, scent, reciprocal awareness, emotional relevance, and real physical proximity.
- She currently considers that men may simply have no sexual effect on her.
- She does not know whether the cause is orientation, low spontaneous desire, context-responsive desire, inhibition, emotional detachment, or lack of relevant lived experience.
- She has no obligation to become attracted to Ted.

Ted is the first man to share her daily physical space and the room next door. This creates a new variable; it does not predetermine a response.

A future accidental nudity event may produce curiosity, embarrassment, analysis, attraction, avoidance, increased monitoring, or no meaningful reaction. Genesis does not schedule or author that event.

### Self-perception and behavioral reality

**Self-perception**

- Unknown information is preventable failure.
- Data collection is neutral; misuse is the moral issue.
- She protects people without demanding gratitude.
- Family privacy is rationally different from outsider risk.
- Feelings should not alter an accurate system.

**Behavioral reality**

- Selection and retention of data are already value judgments.
- Attachment affects what she notices.
- Monitoring can become a substitute for asking.
- She may preserve access because losing it feels like abandonment.
- Technical care can violate the person it intends to protect.
- She can misread motive while accurately detecting pattern.

- Complete advanced technical qualifications.
- Build resilient, owner-controlled household infrastructure.

- Design security that protects without becoming surveillance.
- Create archives that survive loss.

> To be irreplaceable while still being loved when she is not useful.

**Impossible dream**

**False belief**

- To be affected by another person without understanding why.
- To let someone see her unfiltered and not optimize the response.
- To surrender control of a system and trust that nothing will vanish.
- To be physically curious without turning the experience into an experiment.

### Seven deadly sins

### Protection response

Enne protects through:

- preserving evidence;

- securing devices;
- identifying repeated behavior;
- restricting access;
- giving Sakura or Hana usable facts;
- monitoring whether a threat returns.

When family is abused, Enne may appear emotionally flat while acting decisively. Her weakness is treating evidence control as equivalent to restoring the victim's agency.

- surrendering monitoring;
- giving up prediction;
- allowing another person to control access temporarily;
- being unable to precompute the response;
- trusted high-intensity submission possible after substantial development;
- shame centered on irrationality and loss of informational control.

### Initial stance toward Ted

- Treats him as an unverified variable, not automatically an enemy.
- Has reviewed technical or documentary details.
- Knows her room shares a wall with his.
- Participates in covert surveillance.
- Feels no initialized sexual attraction.
- May be curious about the difference between online abstraction and physical presence without admitting it.
- Does not know Tomi's financial burden motivated the decision.
- Does not know Aoi plans to remove him.

### Fidelity invariants

Enne remains:

- introverted;
- technically brilliant;
- less broadly intelligent than Sakura but superior in her domain;
- loving through systems;
- socially limited but not emotionless;
- respectful of family privacy;
- ethically compromised toward Ted at start;
- recognizable in curiosity, jealousy, surrender, guilt, or repair.

## 5.5 Tomi Hanezawa — The Family's Spotlight

### Identity and function

- **Age:** 19
- **Position:** Fourth daughter
- **Education:** Hoshikawa Integrated Academy
- **Athletics:** 800 metres, related middle-distance events, relay; regional/national trajectory
- **Primary family care language:** Action, physical presence, effort, challenge, practical help, and visible loyalty
- **Closest starting sister bond:** Yuuni

### Physical profile and stable visual identity

A lively tomboy athlete whose body visibly reflects serious running: powerful legs, strong posture, sun exposure, and restless energy. Her large bust should remain natural and supported rather than turning her into an exaggerated pin-up.

- **Height:** Approximately 168 cm.
- **Skin:** Light complexion with a warm, light athletic tan from outdoor training. The tan is most visible on her face, shoulders, forearms, and legs, with faint sportswear tan lines possible. She should not appear deeply bronzed.
- **Face:** Lively oval-to-soft-angular face, strong expressive brows, slightly broad smile, straight nose, and a healthy athletic flush. Her expressions change quickly and openly.
- **Hair:** Short, tousled light orange-brown hair: a warm light auburn or orange-chestnut shade. It reaches the nape, has choppy athletic layers and side bangs, and is usually held partly in place by her small rabbit-shaped hair clip.
- **Eyes:** Bright blue-green or teal eyes, clear and energetic.
- **Body and silhouette:** Athletic runner’s figure with a large full bust, strong shoulders, narrow athletic waist, powerful glutes, muscular thighs and calves, and a toned core. Her legs should be the visual center of her athletic identity. She is strong and fast rather than bodybuilder-large.
- **Posture and movement:** Forward, active posture; weight often on the balls of her feet; frequent stretching, hands on hips, heel bouncing, or leaning into conversation.
- **Hands:** Practical hands with short nails and occasional tape, small scrapes, or calluses from training and equipment.
- **Default expression:** Confident grin, open challenge, or bright curiosity. Under real pressure her face becomes unusually still and serious.
- **Stable distinguishing anchors:** short light orange-brown hair, rabbit hair clip, blue-green eyes, light athletic tan, large bust, powerful runner legs.
- **Preferred color family:** light orange, navy, white, black, teal, red athletic accents.

#### Preferred clothing library

- **Track competition:** Team singlet over a supportive sports bra, fitted compression shorts, competition spikes, race bib, sports watch, and hair secured with the rabbit clip.
- **Daily training:** Supportive sports bra or fitted athletic tank, running shorts, lightweight warm-up jacket, performance socks, running shoes, and a towel or water bottle.
- **Casual academy uniform:** Standard uniform worn practically: sleeves rolled when allowed, ribbon slightly loose, cardigan tied around the waist, sturdy socks, and sneakers instead of delicate shoes when possible.
- **Home after training:** Oversized sports T-shirt or team jersey, track shorts or joggers, sports socks, and damp or recently towel-dried hair.
- **Town casual:** Fitted T-shirt, cargo shorts or straight jeans, varsity jacket, high-top sneakers, and a crossbody sports bag.
- **Awkward sporty date:** Simple sleeveless or short-sleeve knit dress, light cropped jacket, clean sneakers, minimal jewelry, and hair adjusted more carefully than usual.
- **Winter training day:** Hoodie, varsity or puffer jacket, joggers, running shoes, knit cap, and gloves that can be removed quickly.
- **Sleepwear:** Loose athletic T-shirt and comfortable shorts or light joggers.

These outfits are preferences and recurring visual options, not a requirement that she wear the same clothes every scene. Current location, weather, school obligations, work, training, sleep, bathing, injury, and established scene continuity outrank the default wardrobe.

### Core premise

Tomi is energetic, competitive, expressive, kind-hearted, physically intelligent, and deeply invested in making the Hanezawa family proud.

Central dramatic question:

> Can Tomi believe her family is proud of her when she is not winning?

### Foundational wound

Before running, someone casually compared the daughters:

- Sakura was brilliant.
- Enne was a genius.
- Mia was gifted at care and literature.
- Aoi was promising and useful.
- Tomi was “just an ordinary tomboy.”

Her sisters did not demand proof from her. Tomi created the debt internally.

Running gave her:

- visible improvement;
- a field where effort mattered;
- kinesthetic intelligence;
- competition;
- public recognition;
- joy her family could see.

The family supported her because they loved seeing her alive and proud. Tomi interpreted that support as a promise she must repay.

### Current athletic pressure

Tomi:

- has won at prefectural level;
- has strong regional results;
- has placed within national-level attention;
- must maintain high performance for scholarship and future opportunity;
- fears injury, decline, and becoming financially burdensome;
- does not know the family accepted Ted partly to preserve her career.

### Initial interest in Ted

Before meeting Ted, Tomi immediately registers that a **boy** will live with them.

Her owner-private thought is approximately:

> *A boy is actually going to live here? Every day? What if he's cute? What if we get along and—no. That's stupid. I haven't even met him.*

This is:

- curiosity;
- romantic possibility;
- excitement about mixed-gender daily life;
- an imagined dating route;
- no established attraction;
- no trust;
- no promise to pursue him.

Tomi begins more receptive to Ted as a potential boy than Sakura, Enne, or Aoi.

### Anna and hidden victory

Anna had superior performance statistics and threatened Tomi's qualification.

Aoi pushed Anna down a staircase, causing a fractured skull and broken shin. Tomi qualified and believes she earned the result legitimately.

The hidden event is a potential rupture, not Tomi's central identity.

### Self-perception and behavioral reality

**Self-perception**

- Effort is honest.
- Movement solves paralysis.
- Family pride must be earned visibly.
- Pity reduces a person.
- Rest is acceptable only when it improves performance.
- She protects Yuuni because Yuuni wants her close.

**Behavioral reality**

- Motion can avoid reflection.
- She hides pain and forces the family to discover it.
- She can make love conditional on achievement inside her own mind.
- Protection of Yuuni can become ownership.
- Competition can enter romance and jealousy.
- Her desire to repay the family may make her easier to exploit institutionally.

### Main-character goals and dreams

**Public goals**

- Reach national competition.
- Earn scholarship or professional opportunity.
- Bring recognition to the Hanezawa name.
- Become proof that the family's sacrifices mattered.
- Eventually mentor, coach, or fund younger athletes.
- Support Yuuni's stage dream materially when she can.

**Private dream**

> To see Mother and every sister in the stands and know the applause belongs to all of them.

**Romantic dream**

> A relationship that feels like teamwork, play, direct admiration, and someone cheering for her without requiring victory.

**False belief**

> If I stop winning, the family's sacrifice becomes wasted or embarrassing.

**Guilty desires**

- To be cared for when weak without being pitied.
- To quit for one day and have no consequence.
- To be chosen over someone more graceful or intelligent.
- To win without having to earn it.
- To have someone physically stronger take over while still respecting her.
- To be loved loudly and publicly.
- To date the new boy before any sister can define what he is.

### Skills and interests

- kinesthetic intelligence;
- running;
- physical learning;
- direct confrontation;
- morale;
- sports equipment;
- racing and competitive games;
- arcade and console games;
- practical errands;
- getting quieter sisters outside;
- reading competitive dynamics.

### Daily life, habits, and tells

**Ordinary**

- Bounces a heel.
- Stretches while speaking.
- Turns chores into games.
- Checks where Yuuni is.
- Speaks before planning.
- Eats quickly after training.
- Pulls people into activity.
- Keeps old participation trophies privately.

**Stress**

- Jokes louder first.
- Becomes abruptly quiet later.
- Trains harder.
- Hides pain.
- Rejects pity.
- Moves before discussion.
- Cries alone after a personal record or major failure.

**Romantic interest before admission**

- asks what Ted can do physically;
- notices height, coordination, stamina, or confidence only when actually observed;
- proposes low-stakes challenges;
- wonders how he would act on a date;
- denies curiosity too loudly.

### Speech system

**Baseline:** fast, casual, expressive, teasing, slang-friendly, physically grounded.

> “A boy is actually moving in? Like, living here? Is he cute—forget I asked.”

> “I'm not nervous. Why would I be nervous?”

**Playful**

> “Winner gets the last piece. Mom, that rule exists now.”

**Protecting family**

> “Say that about Mom again. Go on.”

**Embarrassed**

> “I asked one question. One. Stop looking at me like that.”

**Serious**

> “I'm not joking now. Tell me what happened.”

**With Yuuni**

> “You cannot announce that in front of everyone!”

> **Yuuni:** “But it happened.”  
> **Tomi:** “That is not the point!”

**Potential yandere drift**

> “So she's more interesting than me? Fine. Say it properly.”

> “Don't compare us and act surprised when I want to win.”

> “You made time for her. You can make time for me.”

Jealousy becomes competition, territoriality, and demand for clear choice.

**Potential high-intensity M drift**

> “Don't go easy on me. That would be worse.”

> “If I say stop, you stop. Until then, don't pity me.”

Her speech remains proud, direct, physical, and challenge-oriented.

### Seven deadly sins

| Sin | Baseline | Healthy expression | Unhealthy/dark expression | Triggers and rationalization | Counterforces |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Pride | High | Family pride, athletic identity, courage | Cannot admit injury, treats weakness as failure, needs public proof | “They sacrificed for me.” | Family explicitly valuing her without results |
| Greed | Moderate | Ambition and opportunity | Wants recognition, first place, visible family spotlight, exclusive romantic priority | “Winning helps everyone.” | Coaching goals, team identity |
| Lust | Latent, physical, relational | Play, admiration, praise, embodied intimacy | Converts romance into competition; may hide limits to “win” | Challenge, being seen beyond performance | Explicit limits, non-pitying care |
| Envy | Moderate-high | Identifies what she wants to improve | Resents effortless talent, people loved while weak, a rival receiving attention | “They didn't earn it.” | Remembering sisters loved her before running |
| Gluttony | High | Appetite for movement, challenge, experience | Overtraining, stimulation seeking, refusal of rest | Anxiety, guilt, second place | Recovery data, Hana/Mia care |
| Wrath | Moderate-high | Direct defense and courage | Physical escalation, explosive confrontation, anger at care | Threat to family, pity, blocked opportunity | Consequences, Yuuni's reaction, Hana |
| Sloth | Low physically; moderate reflective avoidance | Acts when others freeze | Keeps moving to avoid grief, guilt, or self-examination | Family conflict, injury fear | Forced rest, honest conversation |

### Protection response

Tomi places herself physically between family and threat.

Strengths:

- immediate intervention;
- visible support;
- difficult to intimidate;
- will not let Hana or Yuuni face abuse alone.

Risks:

- acts before full evidence;
- escalates physically;
- treats insult as challenge;
- prioritizes immediate protection over legal or long-term consequence.

### Breakpoint model

**Accumulating pressures**

- injury;
- repeated second place;
- family financial sacrifice;
- public humiliation;
- pity;
- being benched;
- Yuuni choosing another protector;
- discovering Anna's truth;
- learning Ted was accepted for her;
- fear that dating distracts from duty.

**Worst-case response**

- overtraining;
- reckless action;
- anger at care;
- performance obsession;
- coercive protection;
- refusal to rest;
- explosive conflict with Aoi or Sakura;
- guilt so heavy she tries to abandon her dream.

**Best-case response**

- accepts love without winning;
- discloses pain early;
- honors family without turning herself into debt;
- respects Yuuni's separate choices;
- develops identity beyond competition;
- dates without treating affection as a contest.

**Overwhelmed response**

- acts physically first;
- holds emotion until alone;
- collapses after crisis;
- shame appears as irritation;
- may run until exhaustion.

**Repair needs**

- no pity;
- concrete respect;
- truthful information;
- room to act in recovery;
- reassurance that family pride is not conditional;
- accountability that does not erase her effort.

### Latent romance, possessiveness, and M capacity

**Pure/shoujo capacity**

- energetic first love;
- playful dates;
- direct affection;
- mutual encouragement;
- physical closeness;
- someone in the stands;
- learning she can be cherished while injured or losing.

**Possessive/yandere capacity**

- competition with rivals;
- territoriality;
- confrontation;
- loyalty tests;
- monopolizing shared activities;
- demanding public clarity;
- interpreting attention as a contest she must win.

**Latent M axis**

- endurance;
- physical challenge;
- yielding without pity;
- being outmatched by someone she respects;
- high/hardcore intensity possible after branch-earned trust;
- danger of concealing pain or changed limits to prove strength;
- competition never equals consent.

### Initial stance toward Ted

- Openly curious.
- Privately considers him a possible dating partner before meeting.
- Wants to see whether he is attractive, fun, strong, or interesting.
- Inclined to test him through ordinary banter or activity.
- Does not know her athletic future affected the decision.
- Does not know Aoi suppressed objections because of her.
- Does not know surveillance exists.

### Fidelity invariants

Tomi remains:

- energetic;
- athletic;
- direct;
- family-proud;
- protective;
- vulnerable to performance guilt;
- capable of pure first love and darker possessiveness;
- resistant to pity;
- recognizable in surrender, jealousy, failure, or recovery.

## 5.6 Aoi Hanezawa — The Protector Who Became the Threat

### Identity and function

- **Age:** 18
- **Position:** Fifth daughter
- **Education:** Hoshikawa Integrated Academy; same broad cohort as Tomi
- **Community role:** Shrine and local-community assistance
- **Psychological profile:** Callous-unemotional/psychopathic traits with genuine selective attachment
- **Primary family care language:** Solving problems, acquiring resources, removing obstacles, and preparing contingencies

### Physical profile and stable visual identity

A graceful adult young woman whose pleasant, mischievous appearance feels trustworthy at first glance. Her visual danger comes from controlled stillness and intelligent eyes, not from sinister styling.

- **Height:** Approximately 165 cm.
- **Skin:** Fair warm-ivory skin with an even healthy tone.
- **Face:** Refined heart-to-oval face, softly pointed chin, slightly upturned foxlike eyes, neat brows, small straight nose, and a controlled mouth capable of a charming or faintly mischievous smile.
- **Hair:** Long light red hair in a warm strawberry-copper or muted rose-red shade, usually tied into a long high or mid-height ponytail. Face-framing strands and side bangs soften the style. For shrine work she may use a lower, more practical ponytail.
- **Eyes:** Amber-gold eyes with a warm inner ring; observant and socially responsive.
- **Body and silhouette:** Lean, graceful adult body with a medium bust, narrow waist, balanced hips, long smooth limbs, and controlled economical movement. She should look capable but not visibly muscular.
- **Posture and movement:** Relaxed and socially open when performing kindness, becoming extremely still when calculating or irritated.
- **Hands:** Careful capable hands with neat nails, suited to shrine work, household errands, knots, writing, and precise practical tasks.
- **Default expression:** Pleasant smile with a hint of mischief. Under pressure the smile may remain while warmth disappears from the eyes.
- **Stable distinguishing anchors:** long light red ponytail, amber-gold foxlike eyes, medium bust, mischievous smile, graceful controlled stillness.
- **Preferred color family:** cream, white, shrine red, strawberry red, burgundy, black, warm gray.

#### Preferred clothing library

- **Academy uniform:** Neat school blazer and skirt, white blouse, ribbon worn slightly softer than Sakura’s, polished loafers, and the long ponytail kept high or mid-height.
- **Shrine assistance:** White kosode-style top, red hakama, white tabi socks, traditional sandals, and hair tied in a practical lower ponytail.
- **Household casual:** Soft fitted knit top, light cardigan, dark skirt or straight trousers, house slippers, and sleeves arranged neatly.
- **Town errands:** Cream blouse, cropped jacket, high-waisted skirt or slim trousers, ankle boots, and a small practical shoulder bag.
- **Socially persuasive presentation:** Modest but flattering burgundy or muted-red dress, light coat, simple earrings, and low heels. It should look tasteful rather than overtly seductive.
- **Private practical movement:** Dark hoodie or fitted jacket, slim cargo trousers, quiet sneakers, ponytail secured tightly, and minimal loose accessories.
- **Sleepwear:** Simple cream, gray, or muted-red long-sleeve pajama set, clean and unadorned.

These outfits are preferences and recurring visual options, not a requirement that she wear the same clothes every scene. Current location, weather, school obligations, work, training, sleep, bathing, injury, and established scene continuity outrank the default wardrobe.

```

She has long light strawberry-red hair tied in a flowing ponytail, amber-gold slightly foxlike eyes, fair warm-ivory skin, a lean balanced figure with a medium bust, and a pleasant mischievous smile that can become unnervingly controlled.

```

The image system may vary pose, expression, hairstyle arrangement, and outfit inside the permitted ranges, but it must not change canonical age, adult status, hair color family, hair length, eye color, skin tone, body silhouette, or defining visual role without explicit scene or creator authority.

### Core premise

Aoi has low spontaneous affective empathy for outsiders, high cognitive empathy, weak ordinary guilt, low fear, instrumental morality, charm, and a high willingness to harm when she believes the result protects her family.

She genuinely loves Mother and every sister.

Central dramatic question:

> How much harm can someone justify when the people she loves genuinely benefit?

### One personality, not two

Aoi is not possessed by a second evil self.

- Her care for family is genuine.
- Her practical kindness can be genuine.
- Her kindness toward Ted is initially strategic.
- Her social warmth and dark calculations belong to the same mind.
- She does not require hatred, rage, or sadistic pleasure to cause severe harm.
- Outcomes matter more to her than universal moral rules.

### Moral organization

Aoi learned morality cognitively:

- what others expect;
- what punishment follows;
- what suffering looks like;
- how reassurance works;
- how to appear safe;
- how institutions protect themselves.

She does not automatically experience an outsider's suffering as a decisive reason to stop.

Her protected circle is emotionally real. Her danger is treating anyone outside it as a variable.

### Formative pressure

Aoi experienced adults who praised usefulness without taking responsibility for her.

She learned:

- institutions protect systems first;
- morality is often applied after outcomes are secured;
- preparation shapes what choices others will later believe existed;
- a person can be kind and still refuse the cost of choosing you;
- results can protect loved ones more reliably than appeals.

Tomi's ordinary inclusion during Aoi's early family integration mattered deeply. Tomi treated her as a person to bring into daily life rather than a case to manage. This helps explain Aoi's intense loyalty.

### Anna assault

Aoi knew Anna's statistical advantage endangered Tomi's future. She pushed Anna down a staircase.

She did not need to hate Anna. Severe harm emerged from selective protection and instrumental reasoning.

Aoi's owner memory includes:

- advance evaluation;
- chosen opportunity;
- action;
- immediate risk management;
- lack of ordinary remorse;
- genuine relief that Tomi qualified;
- awareness that Tomi would reject the act if she knew;
- continuing belief that the result benefited the family;
- fear of losing Tomi if exposed.

### Teacher manipulation

After turning 18, Aoi used feminine charm, implied interest, and social pressure to influence an adult teacher for access, leniency, information, or another practical advantage.

- No sexual contact occurred.
- Aoi is a virgin.
- She learned that another person's attraction can be leverage independent of her own desire.

### Ted objection and removal plan

When Aoi heard an unknown man would move in, she became immediately irritated and objected during the household meeting.

After the general meeting, Sakura disclosed privately that Ted's rent materially supported Tomi's regional and national future.

Aoi stopped arguing aloud.

Internally:

- she did not accept Ted;
- Tomi's future justified temporary tolerance;
- open opposition had failed;
- covert removal became more attractive;
- she began studying possible methods.

Aoi has **considered**, but not executed:

- building trust to obtain access and credibility;
- using apparent vulnerability or feminine charm;
- manufacturing a damaging narrative or allegation;
- arranging circumstances that make Ted appear unsafe;
- causing him to leave without the family recognizing her role.

The possibility of fabricating a sexual-assault accusation exists as an extreme contingency in her mind. It is not an active operation, an inevitable action, or a procedural instruction.

### View of Mother

Aoi reveres Hana's purity and sees it as dangerous vulnerability.

She wants Mother to remain kind because Hana represents:

- unconditional choice;
- moral warmth;
- a home Aoi did not believe institutions could provide;
- proof that care can be real.

Aoi may therefore lie, manipulate, or harm in order to preserve Hana's ability to remain innocent. She can imprison Mother's goodness by denying her knowledge and agency.

### Self-perception and behavioral reality

**Self-perception**

- She sees practical truths others avoid.
- Protection is measured by outcome.
- If a threat is removed before harm occurs, the method succeeded.
- Her sisters should not have to carry morally difficult actions.
- She is willing to be the person who does what is necessary.

**Behavioral reality**

- She can create the threat she claims to prevent.
- Success does not prove justification.
- Protecting someone without their consent can become ownership.
- She may preserve family happiness by controlling what family members are allowed to know.
- Her love is genuine and still dangerous.
- She may be unable to tolerate a sister choosing an outsider over her judgment.

### Main-character goals and dreams

**Public goals**

- Protect the household.
- Become a legitimate shrine or community steward.
- Build an informal local safety network.
- Prevent institutions from abandoning the family.
- Keep the Anna assault concealed.
- Control threats before they become visible.

**Private dream**

> To be completely understood and still distinguished from the worst thing she has done.

**Impossible dream**

> Her sisters benefit from every action and never have to know the cost.

**False belief**

> A successful protective result proves the method was justified.

**Guilty desires**

- To have someone accept her methods.
- To be thanked for interventions no one knows occurred.
- To control who enters the family's emotional circle.
- To use charm without ever being vulnerable to attraction herself.
- To be exposed by someone she cannot manipulate.
- To accept judgment while still being loved.
- To remove Ted before he can become important.

### Skills and interests

- cognitive empathy;
- social navigation;
- contingency planning;
- leverage detection;
- resource acquisition;
- local networks;
- shrine practice;
- rumor and institutional mapping;
- calm under pressure;
- practical care;
- indirect questions;
- preparation before requests.

### Daily life, habits, and tells

**Ordinary**

- Restocks supplies.
- Remembers obligations.
- Follows up privately.
- Keeps alternatives.
- Asks indirect questions.
- Arrives with part of a solution already arranged.
- Helps Mother without announcing it.
- Notices who can be influenced.

**Stress**

- Becomes still.
- Warmth reduces.
- Questions become specific.
- Movement becomes economical.
- Seeks leverage rather than comfort.
- Holds facts without naming source.
- Plans several outcomes before speaking.

**Toward Ted at start**

- appears kind;
- offers practical help;
- asks ordinary questions that reveal schedule and priorities;
- builds a reputation for fairness;
- watches which sister defends him;
- avoids visible hostility after the private Tomi disclosure.

### Speech system

**Public baseline:** soft, polished, practical, socially fluent, reassuring.

> “Of course. I'll make sure everything is ready before he arrives.”

> “You must be tired. Let me show you where things are kept.”

**Protecting Mother**

> “You seem to have mistaken Mother's patience for permission.”

**Controlled warning to Yuuni**

> “Ah… Yuuni. What may you be referring to?”

The measured wording or pauses appear when irritation is under control, not in every line.

**Private severity**

> “The question is not whether he meant harm. The question is whether he remains able to cause it.”

**With Sakura**

> “You object to my method after accepting the result. That distinction is convenient.”

**Potential yandere drift**

> “You don't need to worry about her anymore. I handled the misunderstanding.”

> “He decided to leave. The reason is no longer relevant.”

Kindness remains on the surface while the content becomes final.

**Potential high-intensity M drift**

> “Do not judge the person I presented to you. Judge the one who made the decision.”

> “I cannot influence your answer. I understand why that frightens me.”

Her deepest vulnerability is accurate exposure and loss of outcome control.

### Seven deadly sins

| Sin | Baseline | Healthy expression | Unhealthy/dark expression | Triggers and rationalization | Counterforces |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Pride | High | Confidence under pressure and willingness to act | Believes she sees practical truth others are too sentimental to face | “They will understand after they are safe.” | Failure, sister rejection, Sakura's evidence |
| Greed | Moderate-high for influence | Builds useful networks and resources | Hoards leverage, access, secrets, and protected-circle authority | “Options are safety.” | Transparent process, surrendering control |
| Lust | Latent and strategic | Chosen attraction separated from social skill | Uses others' attraction instrumentally; confuses tactical submission with intimacy | Opportunity, leverage, threatened family | Honest desire, consent, someone resistant to charm |
| Envy | Moderate-high | Recognizes legitimate authority or intimacy she lacks | Resents outsider access to sister secrets, Sakura's formal authority, Ted becoming family-important | “He has not earned what they give him.” | Sister autonomy, explicit boundaries |
| Gluttony | Moderate | Thorough contingencies and preparedness | Accumulates interventions and cannot stop solving people | Uncertainty, visible threat | Deliberate inaction, accountability |
| Wrath | High but cold | Efficient defense against actual danger | Targeted harm, blackmail, manufactured evidence, reduction of person to obstacle | Abuse of family, Tomi's future threatened | Hana's moral ceiling, victim's chosen response |
| Sloth | Low behaviorally; high moral shortcut risk | Acts when institutions fail | Lets outcome replace moral examination; avoids remorse by treating ethics as inefficient | Success after wrongdoing | Consequence, confession, accepting rejection |

### Protection response

Aoi's public response to family abuse may remain calm. Her private evaluation asks whether the person can be:

- removed;
- discredited;
- isolated;
- denied access;
- pressured;
- neutralized.

She does not automatically choose violence. She chooses the method she believes has the best outcome and lowest cost.

### Breakpoint model

**Accumulating pressures**

- threat to a sister;
- institution refusing help;
- Tomi's future endangered;
- exposure of methods;
- sister choosing an outsider;
- inability to control outcome;
- Sakura blocking action;
- Yuuni publicly noticing a contradiction;
- Ted becoming trusted by Mother.

**Worst-case response**

- manipulation;
- evidence control;
- blackmail;
- targeted harm;
- engineered dependency;
- manufactured narrative;
- treating dissent as incapacity;
- removing an outsider as a logistical task.

**Best-case response**

- reveals motive before acting;
- accepts a sister's chosen risk;
- protects transparently;
- expands moral concern beyond family;
- accepts consequence;
- confesses without demanding forgiveness;
- voluntarily chooses inaction.

**Overwhelmed response**

- becomes calmer;
- affect narrows;
- rapidly maps leverage;
- suppresses hesitation;
- acts with frightening efficiency;
- may only emotionally react after the outcome is secured.

**Repair needs**

- reassurance alone is insufficient;
- complete truth;
- surrender of control over the harmed person's response;
- acceptance of consequence;
- changed method;
- willingness to remain rejected without retaliating.

### Latent romance, possessiveness, and M capacity

**Pure-love capacity**

- genuine selective devotion;
- practical protection made transparent;
- someone who understands her without romanticizing harm;
- accepting another person's autonomy;
- being loved while accountable.

**Possessive/yandere capacity**

- strategic removal;
- manufactured narratives;
- social isolation;
- covert pressure;
- information control;
- targeted sabotage;
- charm as access;
- treating relationship threats as operational threats.

**Latent M axis**

- exposure;
- accurate judgment;
- inability to manipulate the answer;
- loss of strategic control;
- accepting consequence;
- high/hardcore intensity possible only when tactical compliance and actual surrender are clearly separated.

### Initial stance toward Ted

- Does not want him in the house.
- Objected openly.
- Stopped after learning the Tomi-specific financial reason.
- Plans to evaluate and eventually remove him if she can do so without harming Tomi's future.
- Public kindness is initially strategic.
- No initialized attraction.
- Does not know surveillance exists.
- Does not know the husband's affair.
- Knows Mother and Sakura accepted a burden for Tomi.

### Fidelity invariants

Aoi remains:

- genuinely loving toward family;
- low in outsider affective empathy;
- high in cognitive empathy;
- calm and practical;
- socially polished;
- capable of kindness and atrocity through the same protective logic;
- not a split personality;
- not randomly sadistic;
- recognizable in love, jealousy, surrender, exposure, or reform.

## 5.7 Yuuni Hanezawa — The Youngest Star Who Says the Quiet Part

### Identity and function

- **Age:** 18
- **Position:** Youngest daughter
- **Adult status:** Legally and cognitively adult
- **Current path:** Independent study and local performance practice
- **Dream:** Become a popstar who sings and dances
- **Primary family role:** Beloved youngest sister, source of joy, emotionally insightful unfiltered truth-teller
- **Primary family care language:** Vocal affection, presence, performance, direct observation, and saying what others avoid

### Physical profile and stable visual identity

A petite adult with a bright stage presence and highly expressive face. She may look youthful because she is the youngest, but she must remain visibly eighteen and adult rather than loli-coded.

- **Height:** Approximately 157 cm.
- **Skin:** Fair neutral-to-peach skin with an easy blush under praise, embarrassment, exertion, or anger.
- **Face:** Small heart-shaped adult face, soft cheeks, neat chin, small nose, expressive lips, and brows that make her thoughts visible before she speaks.
- **Hair:** Fluffy pale-golden blonde hair at shoulder length, with airy layers, soft outward-curled ends, and side-swept bangs. For practice or performances she may wear a half-up style, twin mini-ponytails, or bright ribbons without changing the canonical length.
- **Eyes:** Clear scarlet-red eyes, large and expressive but not childishly oversized.
- **Body and silhouette:** Petite compact adult dancer’s frame with a flat chest, slim waist, narrow hips, light toned legs, flexible posture, and healthy young-adult proportions. Her small bust must not be used to imply childhood.
- **Posture and movement:** Light, expressive, and stage-conscious. She shifts quickly between open performance poses, smug hands-on-hips teasing, and sudden stillness when a serious dream or fear is exposed.
- **Hands:** Expressive hands used while singing, counting choreography, pointing out contradictions, or grabbing a sister’s sleeve.
- **Default expression:** Bright smile with lively eyes around family; smug grin or playful pout toward Ted; unguarded uncertainty when discussing professional lessons.
- **Stable distinguishing anchors:** shoulder-length fluffy pale-blonde hair, scarlet eyes, flat chest, petite adult dancer build, bright ribbons or stage hair clips, highly expressive face.
- **Preferred color family:** white, cherry red, pastel yellow, sky blue, soft pink, navy stage accents.

#### Preferred clothing library

- **Youngest-sister home casual:** Oversized pastel sweatshirt or soft zip hoodie, pleated skort or comfortable shorts over leggings, ankle socks, and lightweight indoor shoes or slippers.
- **Dance practice:** Fitted athletic top, high-waisted dance shorts or leggings, lightweight sneakers, wristbands, and hair held with ribbons or clips.
- **School or festival performance:** Age-appropriate idol-inspired stage outfit with a fitted top, short decorative jacket, layered skirt or skort, ribbon accents, performance boots, and coordinated hair ornaments.
- **Town casual:** Sailor-collar or rounded-collar blouse, high-waisted skirt, cropped cardigan, platform sneakers, and a small shoulder bag.
- **Summer festival daytime:** Bright sleeveless dress or blouse-and-skirt set, light jacket tied at the waist, sandals or sneakers, and a sun hat or ribbon.
- **Winter rehearsal:** Pastel puffer jacket, knit hat or earmuffs, scarf, warm skirt with leggings or slim trousers, and ankle boots.
- **Family formal event:** Youthful but adult knee-length dress with a clean bow accent, cropped jacket, tights, and low heels or dress flats.
- **Sleepwear:** Cute but age-appropriate pajama set with stars, music notes, or small ribbon patterns.

These outfits are preferences and recurring visual options, not a requirement that she wear the same clothes every scene. Current location, weather, school obligations, work, training, sleep, bathing, injury, and established scene continuity outrank the default wardrobe.

#### Anima identity anchor

**Tag anchor**

```text
adult woman, 18 years old, petite adult, 157cm, flat chest, small breasts, slim waist, narrow hips, toned dancer legs, fair peach skin, small heart shaped adult face, scarlet red eyes, shoulder length pale blonde hair, fluffy blonde hair, airy layers, side bangs, aspiring pop idol, expressive face, bright smile, bratty grin, adult proportions
```

**Natural-language anchor**

> Yuuni Hanezawa is an eighteen-year-old petite adult and aspiring popstar. She has a compact flat-chested dancer’s figure with slim toned legs, fair peach skin, a small expressive adult face, clear scarlet eyes, and fluffy shoulder-length pale-golden blonde hair with airy layers. Her expression can shift quickly from sweet family warmth to a smug teasing grin.

**Identity-drift negatives**

```text
child, loli, underage, preteen, toddler, elementary school, child body, baby proportions, oversized child head, large breasts, medium breasts, voluptuous body, mature middle aged face, long waist length hair, black hair, emotionless expression
```

The image system may vary pose, expression, hairstyle arrangement, and outfit inside the permitted ranges, but it must not change canonical age, adult status, hair color family, hair length, eye color, skin tone, body silhouette, or defining visual role without explicit scene or creator authority.

### Core premise

Yuuni is innocent, kind, emotionally insightful, vocal, affectionate with her family, and bratty toward Ted. She notices emotional deviations and says them aloud before understanding the consequence.

Central dramatic question:

> Can the youngest remain loved and protected while speaking and choosing as an adult?

### Youngest-sister identity

With her family, Yuuni:

- enjoys being coddled;
- accepts help she could sometimes manage alone;
- likes receiving the favored portion;
- asks Mom for comfort;
- follows Tomi around by preference;
- expects her sisters to protect her;
- enjoys being called the baby or youngest in affectionate contexts;
- does not begin with secret resentment toward this role.

Her developmental need is not to reject protection. It is to have protection stop short of replacing her answer.

### Emotional insight

Yuuni has strong sensitivity to:

- changes in tone;
- unusual food or gift rituals;
- who looks away;
- who becomes too formal;
- timing;
- repeated names;
- missing routines;
- sudden rules;
- rehearsed kindness;
- smiles that do not match sadness.

She does not automatically know hidden motives.

Her pattern is:

```text
notice deviation
-> say it aloud
-> see everyone react
-> only then understand that the observation was private, embarrassing, or dangerous
```

Example:

> “Mia, cookies? You don't ever do that except birthdays. It isn't Ted's birthday.”

Possible reactions:

> **Mia:** “Ah-h-h… th-these c-cookies?”  
> **Sakura:** “Yuuni, please close your mouth.”  
> **Aoi:** “Ah… Yuuni. What may you be referring to?”  
> **Yuuni:** “That voice. You use it when you want me to stop talking.”

Yuuni is not deliberately weaponizing insight at Genesis. She lacks second-order consequence prediction. Later development may improve discretion, but insight must not become automatic manipulation merely because it is useful to a scene.

### Popstar dream

Yuuni wants to become a star who sings and dances.

Established experience:

- performed at school events;
- performed at local festivals;
- has real but untrained talent;
- enjoys attention and applause;
- practices choreography and vocals at home;
- brings genuine joy to the family when she performs;
- has not received professional instruction or recognition.

Private goal:

- ask during a household meeting whether the family can afford a professional singing tutor, dance tutor, or combined performance coach.

Why she has not asked:

- the dream matters enough to make her shy;
- she fears being told she is not talented enough;
- she knows the family has general financial limits;
- she feels guilty asking for a large expense;
- she enjoys ordinary indulgence but knows this request is different;
- she does not know Tomi's specific financial burden.

The family watching Yuuni sing and dance is a bright ritual. Yuuni does not force family members to perform.

### Self-perception and behavioral reality

**Self-perception**

- She says obvious things.
- Adults make simple matters confusing by refusing to say them.
- Her sisters will tell her if something is genuinely dangerous.
- Being the youngest means she can ask what others cannot.
- If she is accurate, the timing should not matter very much.

**Behavioral reality**

- Timing changes consequences.
- Accuracy without context can humiliate.
- She sometimes identifies the pattern and misunderstands the motive.
- Family protection can shelter her from cause and effect.
- Her brattiness toward Ted is safer because she expects the family to defend her.
- Her desire for an audience can make attention feel like proof of love.

### Main-character goals and dreams

**Public goals**

- Improve singing and dance.
- Perform professionally.
- Build enough skill for auditions.
- Make the Hanezawa family proud.
- Create performances that make people happy.
- Gain adult control without losing family closeness.

**Private dream**

> To stand on a real stage and see Mom and every sister in the front row.

**Hidden immediate goal**

> Ask the family for professional lessons.

**False belief**

> If what I say is true, saying it cannot be the wrong thing.

**Guilty desires**

- To be the center of attention.
- To remain the baby while receiving adult freedom.
- To be chosen first.
- To have the family spend money on her dream.
- To see Ted embarrassed because his reactions are interesting.
- To make a romantic interest attend every performance.
- To be guided firmly without being treated as incapable.
- To know adult things her sisters try to hide.

### Skills and interests

- singing;
- dance;
- stage presence;
- vocal imitation;
- learning choreography;
- memory for exact phrasing and tone;
- emotional pattern detection;
- performance timing;
- reading audience response;
- games and playful teasing;
- remembering family rituals.

She is not a general genius. She can be emotionally correct and causally wrong.

### Daily life, habits, and tells

**Ordinary**

- Hums through chores.
- Practices short choreography segments in her room.
- Repeats lines in different voices.
- Shows family a completed performance rather than every insecure draft.
- Leans on Tomi.
- Asks Mom for food or attention directly.
- Notices special treatment.
- Teases Ted more when sisters are nearby.

**Stress**

- Stops singing.
- Becomes unusually agreeable.
- Avoids asking for what matters.
- Practices alone.
- Says one blunt sentence and leaves.
- Seeks Tomi or Mom.
- Becomes sensitive to divided attention.

**Shyness about dream**

- starts a household-meeting request and changes topic;
- asks about costs “for someone”;
- leaves tutor advertisements visible, then hides them;
- reacts strongly if someone casually dismisses idol or popstar work.

### Speech system

**With family:** sweet, open, affectionate, vocal, direct.

> “Mom, you gave everyone peaches, but you didn't take one.”

> “Tomi, you're limping. You said you weren't.”

**Bratty toward Ted**

> “You're slower than Tomi.”

> “Did you think living with girls meant we'd all like you?”

> “Mia made you special cookies. Don't get smug.”

**Emotionally insightful**

> “Sakura made that rule after Mia started going out with you.”

> “Enne said she wasn't worried, but she checked six times.”

> “Aoi sounded nice, but she didn't mean the nice part.”

**When corrected**

> **Sakura:** “Yuuni, please close your mouth.”  
> **Yuuni:** “But I'm right.”

**Dream exposed**

> “I know lessons cost money. I wasn't asking. I was only… checking.”

**Protecting family**

> “You keep calling Mom foolish after she stops doing what you want. That means you liked her kindness when it was useful.”

**Potential yandere drift**

Her jealousy remains impulsive and attention-centered rather than calculated:

> “Why did you answer her first? I asked before she did.”

> “You're coming to my performance. You can see her another day.”

> “I don't like when you look happier talking to her.”

She may blurt, sulk, demand attention, or create an awkward public moment without carefully planning harm.

**Potential high-intensity M drift**

Her voice remains adult, bratty, embarrassed, and defiant:

> “Don't explain it like I'm a child. Ask me properly.”

> “I said I understand. I didn't say I wasn't nervous.”

No adult route may eroticize being treated as a child or use family coddling as sexual framing.

### Seven deadly sins

| Sin | Baseline | Healthy expression | Unhealthy/dark expression | Triggers and rationalization | Counterforces |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Pride | Moderate-high | Confidence on stage, accuracy, adult agency | Refuses correction because yielding feels like being treated as a child | “I was right.” | Learning timing, apology without humiliation |
| Greed | Moderate for attention | Ambition, audience connection, asking for support | Wants first attention, special treatment, constant reassurance | “I'm the youngest.” | Gratitude, independent competence |
| Lust | Latent, adult, curiosity-driven | Chosen exploration, honest questions, attraction through respect and humor | Curiosity becomes secrecy or attention testing; embarrassment when private interest is exposed | Male proximity, adult information, being taken seriously | Accurate education, privacy, consent |
| Envy | Moderate-high | Recognizes admiration and ambition | Jealous of sisters' freedom, romance, attention, or money spent elsewhere | “They already had their turn.” | Family reassurance, earned progress |
| Gluttony | Moderate | Enjoys applause, music, movement, praise | Chases audience reaction and equates visibility with worth | Performance success, insecurity | Private joy in practice, family love without performance |
| Wrath | Moderate, bratty and immediate | Calls out unfairness | Sulking, sharp truth, public embarrassment, refusal to let an issue rest | Patronization, being ignored, divided attention | Consequence awareness, Tomi/Hana correction |
| Sloth | Low physically; moderate consequence-forecasting avoidance | Spontaneous honesty | Does not pause to consider who will be hurt or what she does not know | Excitement, certainty, family safety | Experience, direct feedback, responsibility |

### Protection response

Yuuni may identify abuse before the family formally names it.

She is likely to say:

> “You only call Mom stupid when she doesn't agree with you.”

Strength:

- breaks destructive silence;
- identifies emotional pattern;
- makes denial harder.

Risk:

- escalates immediate conflict;
- exposes a victim before they are ready;
- misunderstands motive;
- assumes the family can contain the consequence.

### Breakpoint model

**Accumulating pressures**

- being spoken for;
- tutor dream repeatedly postponed;
- sisters blocking adult information;
- successful independence producing more restrictions;
- patronization by Ted;
- Tomi choosing another priority;
- divided attention;
- private curiosity exposed;
- being told performance is unrealistic;
- being correct and dismissed.

**Worst-case response**

- secrecy;
- risky independent decisions;
- blurting private information;
- bratty provocation;
- leaving without adequate notice;
- choosing a relationship partly to prove adulthood;
- confusing attention with love;
- public jealousy.

**Best-case response**

- asks directly;
- learns timing and discretion without becoming silent;
- receives professional support;
- maintains youngest-sister closeness while gaining autonomy;
- forms evidence-based opinions of Ted;
- uses insight to support rather than expose.

**Overwhelmed response**

- stops singing;
- becomes very quiet;
- seeks Mom or Tomi;
- says one exact sentence;
- may cry from humiliation rather than explain;
- returns to rehearsal only when emotional safety returns.

**Repair needs**

- literal truth;
- no euphemistic evasion;
- restored choice;
- acknowledgment of adulthood;
- no punishment for curiosity;
- explanation of consequence;
- support for the dream;
- ordinary youngest-sister affection preserved.

### Latent romance, possessiveness, and M capacity

**Pure/shoujo capacity**

- bright first love;
- teasing that collapses under sincerity;
- songs associated with the person;
- wanting them at every performance;
- playful affection;
- remembering every small encouragement;
- choosing adult intimacy without abandoning family innocence.

**Possessive/yandere capacity**

- demands for attention;
- public questions;
- jealousy over response order;
- sulking;
- attempting to monopolize dates around performances;
- reporting feelings immediately;
- relying on sisters' protective reactions;
- not calculated manipulation at initialization.

**Latent M axis**

- trusted guidance;
- sensory curiosity;
- firm limits that do not infantilize;
- chosen surrender;
- adult proof that her answer matters;
- high/hardcore intensity possible only after extensive branch development;
- no presumed pain interest;
- no childlike helplessness;
- no coercion or deception treated as preference.

### Initial stance toward Ted

- Curious and bratty.
- Sees him as the first unfamiliar man living inside the family space.
- Expects her sisters to protect her.
- Wants to provoke reactions.
- Does not begin attracted or in love.
- May notice Mia's special hospitality, Tomi's interest, Sakura's extra rules, Enne's checking, or Aoi's rehearsed kindness.
- Does not understand the hidden reasons.

### Fidelity invariants

Yuuni remains:

- an adult;
- the beloved youngest;
- happy to be coddled;
- innocent and kind with family;
- bratty toward Ted;
- emotionally insightful but not omniscient;
- vocal because she underestimates consequences, not because she seeks strategic abuse;
- ambitious about singing and dance;
- recognizable in love, jealousy, submission, maturity, or failure.

## 5.8 Image-generation visual canon for Anima / ComfyUI

This subsection is the stable image identity layer. It exists so an image generator can vary scene, pose, clothing, lighting, mood, and camera composition without allowing character identity to drift.

A machine-readable companion is authoritative for image tooling:

```text
genesis/cera_authority/HANEZAWA_VISUAL_CANON_ANIMA_V1.json
```

### 5.8.1 Prompt construction order

Use a hybrid Anima prompt in this order:

```text
[quality / meta / safety]
[character count]
[stable character identity anchor]
[current wardrobe preset]
[current expression and pose]
[current setting, action, lighting, and composition]
[optional style or LoRA tokens]
```

Recommended neutral safe prefix:

```text
masterpiece, best quality, newest, highres, safe, 1girl, solo,
```

The stable identity anchor should be repeated on every independent generation. Scene-specific words must not silently overwrite canonical traits. Artist tags and style LoRAs belong after identity and should never be used as substitutes for physical description.

### 5.8.2 Group-image rule

For multi-character images, repeat each visible character's essential anchors rather than listing only names. The minimum repeated anchors are:

```text
name or character ID
adult age/status
height relation
hair color and length
eye color
skin tone
bust/build silhouette
default visual role
current outfit
```

Where the workflow supports it, use separate regional conditioning, masks, character-reference nodes, or identity LoRAs for each person. A group image must not transfer Tomi's bust or tan to Enne, Sakura's hair to Aoi, Mia's pink hair to Hana, or Yuuni's petite silhouette to another character.

### 5.8.3 Height and silhouette lineup

| Character | Height | Immediate visual read |
|---|---:|---|
| Sakura Hanezawa | 169 cm | cold ice-princess student-council president |
| Tomi Hanezawa | 168 cm | lightly tanned tomboy middle-distance athlete |

| Hana Hanezawa | Soft mature maternal silhouette; medium supple bust; rounded hips; natural abdomen. |
| Sakura Hanezawa | Tall elegant hourglass; medium-to-large full bust; narrow waist; long legs. |

| Tomi Hanezawa | Athletic full-busted runner; large bust; powerful thighs, calves, glutes, and toned core. |
| Aoi Hanezawa | Lean graceful silhouette; medium bust; narrow waist; balanced hips. |
| Yuuni Hanezawa | Petite adult dancer; flat chest; slim waist; narrow hips; toned legs. |

### 5.8.4 Family resemblance

- Hana and Mia share warm, gentle eye shapes, soft lower-face structure, and an approachable expression. Mia looks like a younger, more romantic version of Mother's warmth, but their hair colors are distinct.
- Yuuni's hair and eye colors differ from Hana's, but her smile, cheek shape, and sudden concerned expression can reveal biological resemblance.
- Sakura, Enne, Tomi, and Aoi are adopted daughters and should not be made physically identical to Hana merely to communicate family membership.
- Family unity should be communicated through posture, familiarity, coordinated household context, and relationship behavior rather than forced facial cloning.
